                                                 \\\n   147→        if ((err_ptr) != NULL) {                                                  \\\n   148→            char *_sc_msg_buf = NULL; If no format string is\n * required, pass NULL.\n */\n#define SC_RAISE(err_ptr, sc_code, sys, fmt, ...)                                 \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        if ((err_ptr) != NULL) {                                                  \\\n            char *_sc_msg_buf = NULL;                                             \\\n            if ((fmt) != NULL) {                                                  \\\n                /* GNU asprintf allocates; caller must free() later              */\\\n                (void)asprintf(&_sc_msg_buf, fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__);                 \\\n            }                                                                     \\\n            sc_error_set((err_ptr), (sc_code), (sys),                             \\\n                         _sc_msg_buf, __FILE__, __LINE__);                        \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n/*\n * SC_TRY\n * Execute a function returning sc_err_e, propagate on failure while populating\n * the error object with contextual meta-information.\n */\n#define SC_TRY(call, err_ptr)                         \\\n    do {                                              \\\n        sc_err_e _sc_rc = (call);                     \\\n        if (_sc_rc != SC_OK) {                        \\\n            SC_RAISE(err_ptr, _sc_rc, 0, NULL);       \\\n            return _sc_rc;                            \\\n        }                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n\n/*\n * Convenience shortcut: Free the message buffer contained in an sc_error_t,\n * if present, and reset the struct to SC_ERROR_INIT.\n */\nstatic inline void sc_error_reset(sc_error_t *e)\n{\n    if (e == NULL) {\n        return;\n    }\n    if (e->msg != NULL) {\n        free((void *)e->msg);\n    }\n    *e = (sc_error_t)SC_ERROR_INIT;\n}\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n} /* extern \"C\" */\n#endif\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Implementation\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n#ifdef SC_ERRORS_IMPL\n\nstatic const struct {\n    sc_err_e     code;\n    const char  *str;\n} _sc_err_lut[] = {\n    { memory\" },\n    { SC_ERR_NOT_IMPLEMENTED, \"not implemented\" }\n};\n\nconst char *sc_err_str(sc_err_e code)\n{\n    size_t lut_len = sizeof(_sc_err_lut) / sizeof(_sc_err_lut[0]);\n    for (size_t i This compilation unit\n * provides:\n *\n *  • A stable enumeration of error codes (mirrored in sc_errors.h).\n *  • Human-readable translations for each code (thread-safe, async-signal-safe).\n *  • Helper routines / macros for propagating and logging failures.\n *  • A lightweight, per-thread error context similar to errno, but extensible\n *    and service-aware.\n *\n * Copyright 2024\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n */\n\n#include \"sc_errors.h\"        /* public interface */\n#include \"sc_log.h\"           /* internal logging facade */\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <stdatomic.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Private constants / tables\n * ========================================================================== */\n\n/*\n * IMPORTANT: Keep the order in sync with enum sc_error_code in sc_errors.h.\n *\n * DO NOT insert values in the middle – always append, otherwise previously\n * serialized error codes will be reinterpreted.  Mark deprecated entries but\n * keep them in place.\n */\nstatic const char *const g_sc_error_lut[] = {\n    /* 0  */ \"Success\",\n    /* 1  */ \"Unknown failure\",\n    /* 2  */ \"Out of memory\",\n    /* 3  */ \"Null pointer dereference\",\n    /* 4  */ \"Invalid argument\",\n    /* 5  */ \"Index out of range\",\n    /* 6  */ \"Resource not found\",\n    /* 7  */ \"Permission denied\",\n    /* 8  */ \"Operation timed out\",\n    /* 9  */ \"Connection failed\",\n    /* 10 */ \"Protocol violation\",\n    /* 11 */ \"Data corruption detected\",\n    /* 12 */ \"Unimplemented feature\",\n    /* 13 */ \"Unsupported media type\",\n    /* 14 */ \"Configuration error\",\n    /* 15 */ \"I/O failure\",\n    /* 16 */ \"Filesystem full\",\n    /* 17 */ \"GraphQL validation error\",\n    /* 18 */ \"GraphQL execution error\",\n    /* 19 */ \"REST validation error\",\n    /* 20 */ \"Rate limit exceeded\",\n    /* 21 */ \"Service unavailable\",\n    /* 22 */ \"Schema version mismatch\",\n    /* 23 */ \"Database query failed\",\n    /* 24 */ \"Transaction aborted\",\n    /* 25 */ \"Threading error\",\n    /* 26 */ \"Serialization error\",\n    /* 27 */ \"Deserialization error\",\n    /* 28 */ \"Crypto / TLS error\",\n    /* 29 */ \"Cancellation requested\"\n};\n\n_Static_assert(SC_ERR__MAX == (sizeof g_sc_error_lut / sizeof g_sc_error_lut[0]),\n               \"g_sc_error_lut size must match sc_error_code enum\");\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Per-thread errno implementation\n * ========================================================================== */\n\n/*\n * We keep the last error in a thread-local variable to avoid function signatures\n * exploding with additional parameters.  This should NOT be used for control\n * flow.  Instead, each function should return an explicit sc_error_code and let\n * callers inspect that directly.  The TLS value is useful as a “last resort”\n * when integrating with foreign code (e.g., gRPC, pthreads) that predates our\n * conventions.\n */\nstatic _Thread_local sc_error_code tl_last_error = SC_OK;\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nvoid sc_set_error(sc_error_code code)\n{\n    tl_last_error = code;\n}\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nsc_error_code sc_get_error(void)\n{\n    return tl_last_error;\n}\n/* ============================================================================\n * Public translation helpers\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nconst char *sc_error_to_cstr(sc_error_code code)\n{\n    if (code < 0 || code >= SC_ERR__MAX) {\n        return \"Invalid error code\"; /* Should never happen */\n    }\n    return g_sc_error_lut[code];\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*\n * A tiny, header-only logging wrapper could just format the message and forward\n * to sc_log_write().  Here we provide a convenience implementation so callers\n * can do:\n *\n *     if ((rc = sc_foo()) != SC_OK) {\n *         return sc_perror(rc, \"foo failed\");\n *     }\n */\nsc_error_code sc_perror(sc_error_code code, const char *context)\n{\n    const char *msg = sc_error_to_cstr(code);\n\n    if (context && *context) {\n        sc_log_write(SC_LOG_ERROR, \"[%s] %s\", context, msg);\n    } else {\n        sc_log_write(SC_LOG_ERROR, \"%s\", msg);\n    }\n\n    /* stash; do this after logging—handlers may consult sc_get_error() */\n    sc_set_error(code);\n\n    return code;\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Extensibility: custom translators\n * ========================================================================== */\n\n/*\n * Some services may want to register a domain-specific mapping (e.g., map DB\n * vendor error codes into sc_error_code + additional diagnostics).  We expose a\n * simple listener pattern: interested parties register a callback that gets\n * invoked on every sc_set_error() and can perform their own side effects\n * (telemetry, tracing, etc.).\n */\n\n#define MAX_LISTENERS 8\n\ntypedef void (*sc_error_listener_fn)(sc_error_code code);\n\nstatic sc_error_listener_fn g_listeners[MAX_LISTENERS];\nstatic atomic_uint g_listener_count = 0;\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nbool sc_error_add_listener(sc_error_listener_fn fn)\n{\n    if (!fn)\n        return false;\n\n    unsigned idx = atomic_load_explicit(&g_listener_count, memory_order_relaxed);\n\n    if (idx >= MAX_LISTENERS)\n        return false;\n\n    g_listeners[idx] = fn;\n    atomic_fetch_add_explicit(&g_listener_count, 1, memory_order_release);\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nvoid sc_error_notify(sc_error_code code)\n{\n    unsigned count = atomic_load_explicit(&g_listener_count, memory_order_acquire);\n\n    for (unsigned i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        if (g_listeners[i]) {\n            g_listeners[i](code);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Override default setter to broadcast events */\nvoid sc_set_error_broadcast(sc_error_code code)\n{\n    sc_set_error(code);        /* local TLS copy */\n    sc_error_notify(code);     /* fan out to listeners */\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Debug utilities\n * ========================================================================== */\n\n#ifdef SC_BUILD_DEBUG\n\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n\nvoid sc_error_dump(FILE *fp)\n{\n    if (!fp) fp = stderr;\n\n    sc_error_code last = sc_get_error();\n    fprintf(fp, \"[dbg] last error = %d (%s)\\n\",\n            last, sc_error_to_cstr(last));\n}\n\n/*\n * Compile-time check that enum and LUT stay in sync,\n *   *and* catch duplicate values.\n */\nstatic void sc_error_compile_asserts(void)\n{\n    for (int i = 0; i < SC_ERR__MAX; ++i) {\n        /* This branchless trick will abort at run-time if duplicates detected.\n         * Not perfect, but still helps to catch copy-paste mistakes.\n         */\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < SC_ERR__MAX; ++j) {\n            if (strcmp(g_sc_error_lut[i], g_sc_error_lut[j]) == 0) {\n                fprintf(stderr,\n                        \"sc_errors: duplicate error string \\\"%s\\\" @ %d and %d\\n\",\n                        g_sc_error_lut[i], i, j);\n                abort();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n/* Run once at startup */\n__attribute__((constructor))\nstatic void sc_error_init_ctor(void)\n{\n    sc_error_compile_asserts();\n}\n\n#endif /* SC_BUILD_DEBUG */\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * End of file\n * ========================================================================== */\n","numLines":241,"startLine":1,"totalLines":241}}}

* SynestheticCanvas - Palette Service\n * File: palette_service.c\n *\n * This source file implements the Palette Service: a small, embeddable,\n * thread-safe micro-component that handles CRUD operations for color palettes.\n *\n * Responsibilities\n *  – Validation of palette payloads (name, hex codes, etc.).\n *  – In-memory repository via uthash (can be swapped for real DB via adapters).\n *  – Basic monitoring counters (op counts, error counts, latency buckets*).\n *  – Simple pagination for list queries.\n *  – RFC-4122 UUID generation for primary keys.\n *  – Syslog-style logging with compile-time log-level control.\n *\n * NOTE: All public functions are prefixed with palette_service_*\n *\n * ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Build:\n *   cc -std=c11 -DUSE_SYSLOG -lpthread -luuid -o palette_service palette_service.c\n * ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n */\n\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stdarg.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <uuid/uuid.h>\n#include <time.h>\n#include <sys/time.h>\n\n#include \"uthash.h\" /* https://troydhanson.github.io/uthash/ */\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Compile-time configuration\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n#ifndef PALETTE_SERVICE_MAX_COLORS\n#define PALETTE_SERVICE_MAX_COLORS 32\n#endif\n\n#ifndef PALETTE_SERVICE_PAGE_SIZE\n#define PALETTE_SERVICE_PAGE_SIZE 50\n#endif\n\n/* Log levels */\n#define LOG_TRACE  0\n#define LOG_DEBUG  1\n#define LOG_INFO   2\n#define LOG_WARN   3\n#define LOG_ERROR  4\n#define LOG_FATAL  5\n\n#ifndef PALETTE_LOG_LEVEL\n#define PALETTE_LOG_LEVEL LOG_INFO\n#endif\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Logging helpers\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nstatic const char *LOG_LEVEL_NAMES[] = {\n    \"TRACE\", \"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\", \"FATAL\"\n};\n\nstatic void log_internal(int level, const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n#if PALETTE_LOG_LEVEL <= LOG_FATAL\n    if (level < PALETTE_LOG_LEVEL) return;\n\n    va_list args;\n    va_start(args, fmt);\n\n#ifdef USE_SYSLOG\n    /* Map to syslog priorities if desired; omitted for brevity */\n    vfprintf(stderr, fmt, args);\n    fprintf(stderr, \"\\n\");\n#else\n    struct timeval tv;\n    gettimeofday(&tv, NULL);\n    struct tm tm;\n    localtime_r(&tv.tv_sec, &tm);\n\n    char ts[64];\n    strftime(ts, sizeof(ts), \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S\", &tm);\n\n    fprintf(stderr, \"%s.%03ld [%s] \", ts, tv.tv_usec / 1000, LOG_LEVEL_NAMES[level]);\n    vfprintf(stderr, fmt, args);\n    fprintf(stderr, \"\\n\");\n#endif\n\n    va_end(args);\n#endif\n}\n\n#define LOGT(fmt, ...) log_internal(LOG_TRACE, fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGD(fmt, ...) log_internal(LOG_DEBUG, fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGI(fmt, ...) log_internal(LOG_INFO,  fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGW(fmt, ...) log_internal(LOG_WARN,  fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGE(fmt, ...) log_internal(LOG_ERROR, fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOGF(fmt, ...) log_internal(LOG_FATAL, fmt, ##__VA_ARGS__)\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Data types\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\ntypedef struct\n{\n    char  name[32]; /* e.g., \"accent\" */\n    char  hex[8];   /* \"#RRGGBB\" or \"#RGB\" – validated */\n} palette_color_t;\n\ntypedef struct\n{\n    char  id[37];               /* RFC 4122 UUID string */\n    char  name[64];             /* Public-facing name   */\n    unsigned int version;       /* Incremented on update */\n    size_t color_count;\n    palette_color_t colors[PALETTE_SERVICE_MAX_COLORS];\n\n    UT_hash_handle hh;          /* uthash handle (key=id) */\n} palette_record_t;\n\n/* Pagination request and response */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    size_t page_size;       /* defaults to PALETTE_SERVICE_PAGE_SIZE */\n    size_t page_number;     /* 0-based page index                    */\n} palette_page_request_t;\n\ntypedef struct\n{\n    palette_record_t **items; /* dynamic array of ptrs to palette_record_t */\n    size_t item_count;\n    size_t total_items;\n    size_t page_number;\n    size_t total_pages;\n} palette_page_t;\n\n/* Monitoring counters (extremely coarse) */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    unsigned long create_ok;\n    unsigned long create_err;\n    unsigned long read_ok;\n    unsigned long read_err;\n    unsigned long update_ok;\n    unsigned long update_err;\n    unsigned long delete_ok;\n    unsigned long delete_err;\n    unsigned long list_ok;\n    unsigned long list_err;\n} palette_metrics_t;\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Module state\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nstatic struct\n{\n    palette_record_t *records;      /* uthash head */\n    pthread_rwlock_t  lock;\n    bool              initialized;\n    palette_metrics_t metrics;\n} g_ctx = {0};\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Forward declarations\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nstatic bool validate_hex(const char *hex);\nstatic bool validate_palette(const palette_record_t *p, char *err, size_t err_len);\nstatic void free_page(palette_page_t *page);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Public API\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * palette_service_init\n * Initializes internal structures; should be called once on startup.\n */\nbool palette_service_init(void)\n{\n    if (g_ctx.initialized) {\n        LOGW(\"Palette Service already initialized\");\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    if (pthread_rwlock_init(&g_ctx.lock, NULL) != 0) {\n        LOGE(\"Failed to create rwlock\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    g_ctx.records     = NULL;\n    g_ctx.initialized = true;\n    memset(&g_ctx.metrics, 0, sizeof(g_ctx.metrics));\n\n    LOGI(\"Palette Service initialized\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * palette_service_shutdown\n * Frees all allocated resources. Must be idempotent.\n */\nvoid palette_service_shutdown(void)\n{\n    if (!g_ctx.initialized) return;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    palette_record_t *cur, *tmp;\n    HASH_ITER(hh, g_ctx.records, cur, tmp)\n    {\n        HASH_DEL(g_ctx.records, cur);\n        free(cur);\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&g_ctx.lock);\n    g_ctx.initialized = false;\n\n    LOGI(\"Palette Service shut down\");\n}\n\n/**\n * palette_service_create_palette\n * Allocates and stores a new palette. Returns copy of stored record on success.\n */\nbool palette_service_create_palette(const char *name,\n                                    const palette_color_t *colors,\n                                    size_t color_count,\n                                    palette_record_t *out_record)\n{\n    if (!g_ctx.initialized) { LOGE(\"Service not initialized\"); return false; }\n    if (!name || !colors || color_count == 0 || color_count > PALETTE_SERVICE_MAX_COLORS) {\n        g_ctx.metrics.create_err++;\n        LOGW(\"Create request failed validation: invalid arguments\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    palette_record_t *rec = calloc(1, sizeof(*rec));\n    if (!rec) {\n        g_ctx.metrics.create_err++;\n        LOGE(\"Memory allocation failed\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    uuid_t uuid;\n    uuid_generate(uuid);\n    uuid_unparse_lower(uuid, rec->id);\n\n    strncpy(rec->name, name, sizeof(rec->name) - 1);\n    rec->color_count = color_count;\n    rec->version     = 1;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < color_count; ++i)\n        memcpy(&rec->colors[i], &colors[i], sizeof(palette_color_t));\n\n    char err[256];\n    if (!validate_palette(rec, err, sizeof(err))) {\n        g_ctx.metrics.create_err++;\n        free(rec);\n        LOGW(\"Palette validation failed: %s\", err);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Store */\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    HASH_ADD_STR(g_ctx.records, id, rec);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    if (out_record) memcpy(out_record, rec, sizeof(*out_record));\n\n    g_ctx.metrics.create_ok++;\n    LOGI(\"Created palette id=%s (%zu colors)\", rec->id, rec->color_count);\n    return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * palette_service_get_palette_by_id\n * Retrieves a palette by its UUID. Returns false if not found.\n */\nbool palette_service_get_palette_by_id(const char *id, palette_record_t *out_record)\n{\n    if (!g_ctx.initialized) { LOGE(\"Service not initialized\"); return false; }\n    if (!id || !out_record) { g_ctx.metrics.read_err++; return false; }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    palette_record_t *rec = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_ctx.records, id, rec);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    if (!rec) {\n        g_ctx.metrics.read_err++;\n        LOGD(\"Palette id=%s not found\", id);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    memcpy(out_record, rec, sizeof(*out_record));\n    g_ctx.metrics.read_ok++;\n    return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * palette_service_list_palettes\n * Returns a paginated list of palettes. Caller must free page->items.\n */\nbool palette_service_list_palettes(palette_page_request_t req, palette_page_t *out_page)\n{\n    if (!g_ctx.initialized) { LOGE(\"Service not initialized\"); return false; }\n    if (!out_page) { g_ctx.metrics.list_err++; return false; }\n\n    if (req.page_size == 0 || req.page_size > PALETTE_SERVICE_PAGE_SIZE)\n        req.page_size = PALETTE_SERVICE_PAGE_SIZE;\n\n    /* Step 1: snapshot count */\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    size_t total_items = HASH_COUNT(g_ctx.records);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    size_t total_pages = (total_items + req.page_size - 1) / req.page_size;\n    if (req.page_number >= total_pages && total_pages > 0)\n        req.page_number = total_pages - 1;\n\n    /* Step 2: allocate array */\n    palette_record_t **arr = calloc(req.page_size, sizeof(palette_record_t *));\n    if (!arr) {\n        g_ctx.metrics.list_err++;\n        LOGE(\"Memory allocation failed (list)\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Step 3: iterate */\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    palette_record_t *rec, *tmp;\n    size_t idx = 0, skipped = 0, start = req.page_number * req.page_size;\n\n    HASH_ITER(hh, g_ctx.records, rec, tmp)\n    {\n        if (skipped < start) { skipped++; continue; }\n        if (idx >= req.page_size) break;\n        arr[idx++] = rec;\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    /* Build page */\n    out_page->items       = arr;\n    out_page->item_count  = idx;\n    out_page->total_items = total_items;\n    out_page->page_number = req.page_number;\n    out_page->total_pages = total_pages;\n\n    g_ctx.metrics.list_ok++;\n    return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * palette_service_update_palette\n * Replaces palette data atomically; increments version.\n */\nbool palette_service_update_palette(const char *id,\n                                    const char *name,\n                                    const palette_color_t *colors,\n                                    size_t color_count,\n                                    palette_record_t *out_record)\n{\n    if (!g_ctx.initialized) { LOGE(\"Service not initialized\"); return false; }\n    if (!id || !name || !colors || color_count == 0 ||\n        color_count > PALETTE_SERVICE_MAX_COLORS) {\n        g_ctx.metrics.update_err++;\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    palette_record_t *rec = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_ctx.records, id, rec);\n    if (!rec) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n        g_ctx.metrics.update_err++;\n        LOGD(\"Palette id=%s not found for update\", id);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    strncpy(rec->name, name, sizeof(rec->name) - 1);\n    rec->color_count = color_count;\n    rec->version++;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < color_count; ++i)\n        memcpy(&rec->colors[i], &colors[i], sizeof(palette_color_t));\n\n    char err[256];\n    if (!validate_palette(rec, err, sizeof(err))) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n        g_ctx.metrics.update_err++;\n        LOGW(\"Palette update validation failed: %s\", err);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    if (out_record) memcpy(out_record, rec, sizeof(*out_record));\n\n    g_ctx.metrics.update_ok++;\n    LOGI(\"Updated palette id=%s version=%u\", rec->id, rec->version);\n    return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * palette_service_delete_palette\n * Removes palette from storage.\n */\nbool palette_service_delete_palette(const char *id)\n{\n    if (!g_ctx.initialized) { LOGE(\"Service not initialized\"); return false; }\n    if (!id) { g_ctx.metrics.delete_err++; return false; }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    palette_record_t *rec = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_ctx.records, id, rec);\n    if (rec) {\n        HASH_DEL(g_ctx.records, rec);\n        free(rec);\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n        g_ctx.metrics.delete_ok++;\n        LOGI(\"Deleted palette id=%s\", id);\n        return true;\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    g_ctx.metrics.delete_err++;\n    LOGD(\"Palette id=%s not found for deletion\", id);\n    return false;\n}\n\n/**\n * palette_service_dump_metrics\n * Caller provides preallocated struct. Thread-safe read.\n */\nvoid palette_service_dump_metrics(palette_metrics_t *out_metrics)\n{\n    if (!out_metrics) return;\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    memcpy(out_metrics, &g_ctx.metrics, sizeof(*out_metrics));\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Validation helpers (static)\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic bool validate_hex(const char *hex)\n{\n    if (!hex) return false;\n    size_t len = strlen(hex);\n    if (!(len == 4 || len == 7)) return false;  /* \"#RGB\" or \"#RRGGBB\" */\n\n    if (hex[0] != '#') return false;\n\n    for (size_t i = 1; i < len; ++i) {\n        char c = hex[i];\n        if (!((c >= '0' && c <= '9') ||\n              (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F') ||\n              (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f')))\n            return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic bool validate_palette(const palette_record_t *p, char *err, size_t err_len)\n{\n    if (!p) { snprintf(err, err_len, \"palette pointer null\"); return false; }\n    if (strlen(p->name) == 0) {\n        snprintf(err, err_len, \"empty name\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (p->color_count == 0 || p->color_count > PALETTE_SERVICE_MAX_COLORS) {\n        snprintf(err, err_len, \"invalid color count\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < p->color_count; ++i) {\n        const palette_color_t *c = &p->colors[i];\n        if (strlen(c->name) == 0) {\n            snprintf(err, err_len, \"color %zu has empty name\", i);\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (!validate_hex(c->hex)) {\n            snprintf(err, err_len, \"color %zu has invalid hex '%s'\", i, c->hex);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Utility\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void free_page(palette_page_t *page)\n{\n    if (!page) return;\n    free(page->items);\n    memset(page, 0, sizeof(*page));\n}\n\n/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Example usage (unit test stub) – compile with -DPALETTE_SERVICE_TEST\n * It is intended to be\n * wired into whichever framework the deployment chooses\n * (REST gateway, GraphQL resolver, message-queue worker,\n * etc.).  All external I/O is expressed through abstract\n * request / response objects so the handler stays\n * decoupled from higher layers.\n *\n * Author: SynestheticCanvas Core Team\n * License: MIT\n */\n\n#include \"palette_handler.h\"\n\n#include <assert.h>\n#include <ctype.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n#include \"config.h\"             /* service-wide configuration */\n#include \"logger.h\"             /* structured logging facade  */\n#include \"metrics.h\"            /* Prometheus/StatsD exporter */\n#include \"palette_repository.h\" /* storage adapter            */\n#include \"validation.h\"         /* request validation helpers */\n\n#include \"cjson/cJSON.h\"        /* third-party JSON lib       */\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Internal helpers                                                         */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/*\n * Convert a Palette domain object into a cJSON representation.\n */\nstatic cJSON *palette_to_json(const Palette *palette)\n{\n    assert(palette != NULL);\n\n    cJSON *json = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    if (!json)\n        return NULL;\n\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(json, \"id\", palette->id);\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(json, \"name\", palette->name);\n\n    cJSON *colors = cJSON_AddArrayToObject(json, \"colors\");\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < palette->color_count; ++i)\n    {\n        cJSON_AddItemToArray(colors, cJSON_CreateString(palette->colors[i]));\n    }\n\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(json, \"createdAt\", palette->created_at_iso8601);\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(json, \"updatedAt\", palette->updated_at_iso8601);\n\n    return json;\n}\n\n/*\n * Parse query parameters ?page=<n>&size=<m>.  Defaults are taken from\n * configuration if params are missing.  Returns 0 on success, -1 on failure.\n */\nstatic int parse_pagination(const HttpRequest *req,\n                            Pagination       *out_pg,\n                            char            **error_msg)\n{\n    assert(req != NULL);\n    assert(out_pg != NULL);\n\n    const char *page_str = http_request_query_param(req, \"page\");\n    const char *size_str = http_request_query_param(req, \"size\");\n\n    uint32_t page = page_str ? (uint32_t)strtoul(page_str, NULL, 10)\n                             : CONFIG_DEFAULT_PAGE;\n    uint32_t size = size_str ? (uint32_t)strtoul(size_str, NULL, 10)\n                             : CONFIG_DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE;\n\n    if (page == 0)\n        page = CONFIG_DEFAULT_PAGE; /* page numbers start at 1 */\n\n    if (size == 0 || size > CONFIG_MAX_PAGE_SIZE)\n    {\n        asprintf(error_msg,\n                 \"Page size must be between 1 and %u\",\n                 CONFIG_MAX_PAGE_SIZE);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    out_pg->page = page;\n    out_pg->size = size;\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/*\n * Push JSON to HttpResponse and handle memory.\n */\nstatic int json_to_response(HttpResponse *resp, cJSON *json, int status_code)\n{\n    char *payload = cJSON_PrintUnformatted(json);\n    if (!payload)\n        return -1;\n\n    http_response_set_status(resp, status_code);\n    http_response_set_header(resp, \"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    http_response_set_body(resp, payload, strlen(payload));\n\n    free(payload);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Public API                                                               */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nPaletteHandler *palette_handler_create(PaletteRepository *repo,\n                                       Logger            *logger,\n                                       MetricsRegistry   *metrics,\n                                       const char        *service_version)\n{\n    assert(repo && logger && metrics && service_version);\n\n    PaletteHandler *handler = calloc(1, sizeof *handler);\n    if (!handler)\n        return NULL;\n\n    handler->repo            = repo;\n    handler->log             = logger;\n    handler->metrics         = metrics;\n    handler->service_version = strdup(service_version);\n    if (!handler->service_version)\n    {\n        free(handler);\n        return NULL;\n palette_handler_handle_healthz(PaletteHandler    *handler,\n                                    const HttpRequest *req,\n                                    HttpResponse      *resp)\n{\n    (void)req; /* unused */\n\n    cJSON *root = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(root, \"status\", \"ok\");\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(root, \"service\", \"palette\");\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(root, \"version\", handler->service_version);\n\n    json_to_response(resp, root, HTTP_STATUS_OK);\n    cJSON_Delete(root);\n\n    logger_info(handler->log, \"healthz\", \"Served health check\");\n}\n\nvoid palette_handler_handle_get_list(PaletteHandler    *handler,\n                                     const HttpRequest *req,\n                                     HttpResponse      *resp)\n{\n    Pagination pg = {0};\n    char *pg_err  = NULL;\n\n    if (parse_pagination(req, &pg, &pg_err) < 0)\n    {\n        logger_warn(handler->log, \"validation\",\n                    \"Invalid pagination params: %s\", pg_err);\n        http_response_send_error(resp, HTTP_STATUS_BAD_REQUEST, pg_err);\n        free(pg_err);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    RepositoryResult *repo_res = palette_repository_fetch_page(\n        handler->repo, pg.page, pg.size);\n\n    if (repo_res->error)\n    {\n        logger_error(handler->log, \"repository\",\n                     \"Failed to fetch palettes: %s\", repo_res->error);\n        http_response_send_error(resp, HTTP_STATUS_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n                                 \"Internal server error\");\n        repository_result_free(repo_res);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cJSON *root = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    cJSON *arr  = cJSON_AddArrayToObject(root, \"items\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < repo_res->palettes_len; ++i)\n    {\n        cJSON *jpal = palette_to_json(repo_res->palettes[i]);\n        cJSON_AddItemToArray(arr, jpal);\n    }\n\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(root, \"page\", json_to_response(resp, root, HTTP_STATUS_OK);\n\n    cJSON_Delete(root);\n    repository_result_free(repo_res);\n\n    metrics_inc_counter(handler->metrics,\n                        \"palette_list_requests_total\", 1);\n}\n\nvoid palette_handler_handle_get_single(PaletteHandler    *handler,\n                                       const HttpRequest *req,\n                                       HttpResponse      *resp,\n                                       const char        *palette_id)\n{\n    if (!validation_is_uuid(palette_id))\n    {\n        http_response_send_error(resp, http_response_send_error(resp, HTTP_STATUS_BAD_REQUEST, val_error);\n        free(val_error);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cJSON *json = cJSON_ParseWithLength(body, body_len);\n    if (!json)\n    {\n        http_response_send_error(resp, HTTP_STATUS_BAD_REQUEST,\n                                 \"Malformed JSON\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const char *name = cJSON_GetStringValue(cJSON_GetObjectItem(json, \"name\"));\n    cJSON      *col  = cJSON_GetObjectItem(json, \"colors\");\n\n    size_t color_count = (size_t)cJSON_GetArraySize(col);\n    char               \"Memory allocation failed\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < color_count; ++i)\n    {\n        const char *cstr = cJSON_GetStringValue(\n            cJSON_GetArrayItem(col, (int)i));\n        colors[i] = strdup(cstr);\n    }\n\n    PaletteCreateSpec spec = {\n        .name        = name,\n        .colors      = colors,\n        .color_count = color_count,\n    };\n\n    Palette *created = palette_repository_create(handler->repo, &spec);\n    palette_create_spec_free(&spec); /* frees colors list */\n\n    if (!created)\n    {\n        cJSON_Delete(json);\n        http_response_send_error(resp, HTTP_STATUS_CONFLICT,\n                                 \"Palette with the same name already exists\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cJSON *resp_json = palette_to_json(created);\n    json_to_response(resp, resp_json, HTTP_STATUS_CREATED);\n\n    cJSON_Delete(resp_json);\n    cJSON_Delete(json);\n    palette_free(created);\n\n    metrics_inc_counter(handler->metrics,\n                        \"palette_create_requests_total\", 1);\n}\n\nvoid palette_handler_handle_delete(PaletteHandler    *handler,\n }\n\n    if (!palette_repository_delete(handler->repo, palette_id))\n    {\n        http_response_send_error(resp, HTTP_STATUS_NOT_FOUND,\n                                 \"Palette not found\");\n (float)RAND_MAX;\n}\n\n/* Basic gradient noise (returns value in [0;1]) */\nstatic void\ngenerate_noise_texture(uint32_t width, uint32_t height,\n                       float frequency, uint8_t *out_pixels)\n{\n    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < height; ++y) {\n        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {\n            float nx = x * frequency;\n            float ny = y * frequency;\n            /* pseudo-random value per pixel */\n            float v = fabsf(sinf(nx * 12.9898f + ny * 78.233f) * 43758.5453f);\n            v = v - floorf(v); /* fract */\n\n            uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(v * 255.0f);\n            size_t idx = ((size_t)y * width + x) * 4;\n            out_pixels[idx + 0] = c;  /* R */\n            out_pixels[idx + 1] = c;  /* G */\n            out_pixels[idx + 2] = c;  /* B */\n            out_pixels[idx + 3] = 255;/* A opaque */\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * LRU Cache\n *    entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n    if (entry->next)\n        entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n    if (g_cache.tail == entry)\n        g_cache.tail = entry->prev;\n\n    /* insert at front */\n victim->meta.id, (uint64_t)(g_cache.bytes / 1024));\n\n        free(victim->pixels);\n        free(victim);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void\ncache_insert(cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    entry->prev = NULL;\n *method;\n} rest_ctx_t;\n\nstatic int\nresp_json(struct MHD_Connection *conn, unsigned status,\n          const char *json_str)\n{\n    struct MHD_Response *resp = MHD_create_response_from_buffer(\n        strlen(json_str), (void *)json_str, MHD_RESPMEM_MUST_COPY);\n    if (!resp)\n        return MHD_NO;\n\n    MHD_add_response_header(resp, \"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    int ret = Converts an incoming request into an internal\n * representation, performs route lookup, validation, and hands control to the\n * endpoint handler.  Returns `true` if a route was matched (regardless of\n * HTTP status).  Returns `false` when no route matched, allowing the caller to\n * fall back to 404 behavior.\n */\nbool sc_router_handle(sc_router_t        *router,\n                      const sc_http_request_t  *req,\n                      sc_http_response_t       *res)\n{\n    if (!router || !req || !res)\n        return false;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < router->len; ++i)\n    {\n        const route_t *route = &router->routes[i];\n\n        if (route->method != req->method ||\n            route->version != req->version)\n            continue;\n\n        if (regexec(&route->regex, req->path, 0, NULL, 0) != 0)\n            continue; /* path mismatch */\n\n        /* --- matched --- */\n        char validation_err[MAX_VALIDATION_ERR] = {0};\n        if (route->validator &&\n            !route->validator(req, validation_err, sizeof(validation_err)))\n        {\n            sc_response_set_status(res, 400);\n            sc_response_set_body_fmt(res, \"Validation failed: %s\",\n                                     validation_err[0] ? validation_err\n                                                       : \"Schema mismatch\");\n            if (router->enable_logging)\n                syslog(LOG_WARNING, \"Validation failed for %s %s: %s\",\n                       sc_http_method_name(req->method),\n                       req->path,\n                       validation_err);\n            return true; /* handled */\n        }\n\n        sc_param_list_t params;\n        sc_param_list_init(&params);\n        if (!extract_params(route, req->path, &params))\n        {\n            sc_response_set_status(res, 500);\n            sc_response_set_body(res, \"Internal error (param extraction)\");\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        /* Pagination helper — attaches to request struct for convenience */\n        sc_pagination_t pagination;\n        sc_pagination_from_query(req->query, DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE, &pagination);\n\n        if (router->enable_logging)\n            syslog(LOG_INFO, \"Dispatch %s %s → %s (page=%u, size=%u)\",\n                   sc_http_method_name(req->method),\n                   req->path,\n                   route->pattern,\n                   pagination.page,\n                   pagination.page_size);\n\n        /* Call user handler */\n        bool ok = route->handler(req, res, &params, &pagination, route->user_ctx);\n\n        sc_param_list_free(&params);\n        return ok;\n    }\n\n    return false; /* no route matched */\n}\n\n/*─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Pattern compilation + helpers\n *─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * Convert a human-readable pattern (e.g. \"/palette/:id\") into a POSIX regex,\n * capturing each named parameter.  For every \":param\", we generate a \"([^/]+)\"\n * capture group and store \"param\" into the route's `param_names`.\n */\nstatic bool compile_pattern(route_t *r, const char *pattern,\n                            char *err_buf, size_t err_len)\n{\n    char regex_buf[ROUTER_MAX_REGEX] = {0};\n    char *dst = regex_buf;\n    const char *src = pattern;\n\n    if (*src != '/')\n    {\n        snprintf(err_buf, err_len, \"Pattern must start with '/'\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    *(dst++) = '^'; /* match from start */\n\n    while (*src && (dst - regex_buf) < ROUTER_MAX_REGEX - 1)\n    {\n        if (*src == ':')\n        {\n            /* start of param name */\n            const char *start = ++src;\n            while (*src && (isalnum(*src) || *src == '_'))\n                ++src;\n\n            size_t len = src - start;\n            if (len == 0)\n            {\n                snprintf(err_buf, err_len, \"Empty param name in pattern\");\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            char name[64];\n            memcpy(name, start, len);\n            name[len] = '\\0';\n            strvec_push(&r->param_names, name);\n\n            /* append capture group */\n            strcpy(dst, \"([^/]+)\");\n            dst += strlen(\"([^/]+)\");\n            continue;\n        }\n        else if (*src == '*')\n        {\n            /* greedy wildcard */\n            strcpy(dst, \"(.*)\");\n            dst += strlen(\"(.*)\");\n            ++src;\n            continue;\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            /* escape regex meta-characters */\n            if (strchr(\".+?^$()[]{}\", *src))\n                *(dst++) = '\\\\';\n            *(dst++) = *src++;\n        }\n    }\n\n    *(dst++) = '$';\n    *dst = '\\0';\n\n    int rc = regcomp(&r->regex, regex_buf, REG_EXTENDED | REG_ICASE);\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        regerror(rc, &r->regex, err_buf, err_len);\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* Extract params into key/value vector (already matched!) */\nstatic bool extract_params(const route_t   *route,\n                           const char      *path,\n                           sc_param_list_t *out)\n{\n    size_t groups   = route->param_names.len;\n    regmatch_t      match[groups + 1]; /* group 0 = whole match */\n\n    if (regexec(&route->regex, path, groups + 1, match, 0) != 0)\n        return false;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < groups; ++i)\n    {\n        if (match[i + 1].rm_so == -1)\n            continue; /* shouldn't happen */\n\n        size_t len = (size_t)(match[i + 1].rm_eo - match[i + 1].rm_so);\n        char *value = strndup(path + match[i + 1].rm_so, len);\n        sc_param_list_add(out, route->param_names.items[i], value);\n        free(value);\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic void route_destroy(route_t *r)\n{\n    if (!r) return;\n\n    regfree(&r->regex);\n    free(r->pattern);\n    strvec_free(&r->param_names);\n}\n\n/*─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Minimal helper implementations (could be moved to headers)\n *─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/* Human-readable method names */\nconst char *sc_http_method_name(sc_http_method_e m)\n{\n    switch (m)\n    {\n        case SC_HTTP_GET:     return \"GET\";\n        case SC_HTTP_POST:    return \"POST\";\n        case SC_HTTP_PUT:     return \"PUT\";\n        case SC_HTTP_PATCH:   return \"PATCH\";\n        case SC_HTTP_DELETE:  return \"DELETE\";\n        default:              return \"UNKNOWN\";\n    }\n}\n\n/* default pagination implementation if not linked */\n#ifndef HAVE_PAGINATION_UTILS\nvoid sc_pagination_from_query(const char *qs, unsigned default_size,\n                              sc_pagination_t *out)\n{\n    out->page_size = default_size;\n    out->page      = 1;\n\n    if (!qs) return;\n\n    char *dup = strdup(qs);\n    char *token, *saveptr;\n    for (token = strtok_r(dup, \"&\", &saveptr);\n         token;\n         token = strtok_r(NULL, \"&\", &saveptr))\n    {\n        if (strncmp(token, \"page_size=\", 10) == 0)\n            out->page_size = (unsigned) atoi(token + 10);\n        else if (strncmp(token, \"page=\", 5) == 0)\n            out->page = (unsigned) atoi(token + 5);\n    }\n    free(dup);\n}\n#endif\n```","numLines":400,"startLine":1,"totalLines":400}}}

Simple static thread pool (one per service or limited by parallelism) */\n    size_t workers = ctx->count < (size_t)ctx->parallelism ? ctx->count\n                                                           : (size_t)ctx->parallelism;\n    pthread_t *threads = calloc(workers, sizeof *threads);\n\n    size_t next_job = 0;\n    size_t active   = 0;\n\n    while (next_job < ctx->count || active) {\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01FnJrGQHFUZjAzxkNdXR8PM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Mx3hv9nSdQ5EUVPTKh94cE","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Solution: Refactor Service-Level Error Handling into a Unified Common Library Abstraction\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document provides a complete solution for centralizing and standardizing error handling across the SynestheticCanvas microservice suite. The refactoring involves:\n\n1. **Enhancing the Common Error Abstraction** in `libs/sc_common/`\n2. **Refactoring the Palette Service** (`services/palette-service/`)\n3. **Refactoring the Texture Service** (`services/texture-service/`)\n4. **Updating the API Gateway** (`api-gateway/`)\n5. **Updating Unit Tests**\n\n## Analysis of Existing Error Handling\n\n### Current State\n\nAfter analyzing the codebase, I found the following error handling patterns:\n\n1. **sc_common library** (`libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h`):\n   - Already has an `sc_error_t` struct with `code`, `sys_err`, `msg`, `file`, `line`\n   - Has an `sc_err_e` enumeration with comprehensive error codes\n   - Has an `sc_err_to_http()` function that maps error codes to HTTP status codes\n   - Uses macros like `SC_RAISE()` and `SC_TRY()` for error propagation\n   - **Missing**: No service domain identification, no factory functions for dynamic error creation\n\n2. **Palette Service** (`services/palette-service/`):\n   - Uses `bool` return values for most functions\n   - Has service-specific status enum `sc_ps_status_t` in the public header\n   - Repository uses `palette_repo_status_t` enum\n   - No unified error message propagation\n\n3. **Texture Service** (`services/texture-service/`):\n   - Uses `texture_service_status_t` enum\n   - Repository uses integer codes (`REPO_OK`, `REPO_ERR_*`)\n   - No unified error message propagation\n\n4. **API Gateway** (`api-gateway/`):\n   - Service client uses simple integer return codes\n   - Fallback handlers generate JSON error responses manually\n   - No structured consumption of service errors\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### 1. Enhanced Common Error Abstraction\n\n#### Changes to `libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h`\n\nAdd the following after the existing includes and before the error enumeration:\n\n```c\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Service domain enumeration\n * Identifies the originating service for unified error handling across\n * the microservice constellation.\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef enum {\n    SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN = 0,\n    SC_DOMAIN_COMMON,         /* Common library errors */\n    SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE,        /* Palette service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_TEXTURE,        /* Texture service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_AUDIO,          /* Audio service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_NARRATIVE,      /* Narrative service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY         /* API Gateway */\n} sc_service_domain_t;\n```\n\nModify the existing `sc_error_t` struct to include domain and HTTP status:\n\n```c\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Structured error object (enhanced)\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef struct sc_error {\n    sc_err_e             code;            /* High level error code */\n    int                  sys_err;         /* Underlying errno / subsystem code */\n    sc_service_domain_t  domain;          /* Originating service domain */\n    int                  http_status_code;/* Recommended HTTP status code */\n    char                *message;         /* Dynamically allocated, human-readable text */\n    const char          *file;            /* Source file where error was raised */\n    int                  line;            /* Line in source file */\n} sc_error_t;\n\n#define SC_ERROR_INIT { \\\n    .code = SC_OK, \\\n    .sys_err = 0, \\\n    .domain = SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN, \\\n    .http_status_code = 200, \\\n    .message = NULL, \\\n    .file = NULL, \\\n    .line = 0 \\\n}\n```\n\nAdd factory and destructor function declarations:\n\n```c\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Error factory and destructor functions\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/**\n * sc_error_create\n *\n * Factory function to create a new sc_error_t instance with all fields populated.\n * The message is formatted using printf-style variadic arguments.\n *\n * @param domain      The originating service domain\n * @param code        The service-specific error code\n * @param http_status The recommended HTTP status code to return\n * @param format      Printf-style format string for the error message\n * @param ...         Format arguments\n *\n * @return Pointer to a heap-allocated sc_error_t, or NULL on allocation failure.\n *         Caller must free with sc_error_destroy().\n */\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                            int code,\n                            int http_status,\n                            const char* format, ...) SC_ATTR_FORMAT(printf, 4, 5);\n\n/**\n * sc_error_create_v\n *\n * Variadic list version of sc_error_create for internal use.\n */\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create_v(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                              int code,\n                              int http_status,\n                              const char* format,\n                              va_list args);\n\n/**\n * sc_error_destroy\n *\n * Frees all resources associated with an sc_error_t instance.\n * Safe to call with NULL.\n *\n * @param err The error to destroy\n */\nvoid sc_error_destroy(sc_error_t* err);\n\n/**\n * sc_error_get_domain_name\n *\n * Returns a human-readable string for the service domain.\n */\nconst char* sc_error_get_domain_name(sc_service_domain_t domain);\n```\n\n#### Implementation in `libs/sc_common/src/sc_errors.c`\n\nAdd implementations after the existing code:\n\n```c\n/* ============================================================================\n * Domain name lookup table\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nstatic const char* const g_domain_names[] = {\n    [SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN]   = \"unknown\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_COMMON]    = \"common\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE]   = \"palette-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_TEXTURE]   = \"texture-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_AUDIO]     = \"audio-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_NARRATIVE] = \"narrative-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY]   = \"api-gateway\"\n};\n\nconst char* sc_error_get_domain_name(sc_service_domain_t domain)\n{\n    if (domain < 0 || domain > SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY) {\n        return \"invalid-domain\";\n    }\n    return g_domain_names[domain];\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Error factory implementation\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create_v(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                              int code,\n                              int http_status,\n                              const char* format,\n                              va_list args)\n{\n    sc_error_t* err = calloc(1, sizeof(sc_error_t));\n    if (!err) {\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    err->domain = domain;\n    err->code = (sc_err_e)code;\n    err->http_status_code = http_status;\n    err->sys_err = errno;  /* Capture current errno */\n    err->file = NULL;\n    err->line = 0;\n\n    /* Format the message if format string provided */\n    if (format != NULL) {\n        va_list args_copy;\n        va_copy(args_copy, args);\n        int len = vsnprintf(NULL, 0, format, args_copy);\n        va_end(args_copy);\n\n        if (len > 0) {\n            err->message = malloc((size_t)len + 1);\n            if (err->message) {\n                vsnprintf(err->message, (size_t)len + 1, format, args);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return err;\n}\n\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                            int code,\n                            int http_status,\n                            const char* format, ...)\n{\n    va_list args;\n    va_start(args, format);\n    sc_error_t* err = sc_error_create_v(domain, code, http_status, format, args);\n    va_end(args);\n    return err;\n}\n\nvoid sc_error_destroy(sc_error_t* err)\n{\n    if (err == NULL) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Free the dynamically allocated message */\n    if (err->message != NULL) {\n        free(err->message);\n        err->message = NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Free the error struct itself */\n    free(err);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. API Gateway Refactoring\n\n#### Changes to `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.h`\n\nAdd support for structured errors:\n\n```c\n#include \"sc_errors.h\"\n\n/**\n * Response structure that includes error details\n */\ntypedef struct sc_service_response {\n    int         status;         /* SC_OK or error code */\n    long        http_status;    /* HTTP status code */\n    cJSON      *body;           /* Response body (JSON) */\n    sc_error_t *error;          /* Error details if status != SC_OK */\n} sc_service_response_t;\n\n/**\n * Free a service response\n */\nvoid sc_service_response_free(sc_service_response_t *resp);\n\n/**\n * Enhanced call function that returns structured errors\n */\nint sc_service_client_call_ex(sc_service_client_t     *cli,\n                              sc_service_endp_t        endpoint,\n                              const char              *method,\n                              const char              *path,\n                              const char              *request_json,\n                              sc_service_response_t   *response);\n```\n\n#### Changes to `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c`\n\n```c\n#include \"sc_errors.h\"\n\nvoid sc_service_response_free(sc_service_response_t *resp)\n{\n    if (!resp) return;\n\n    if (resp->body) {\n        cJSON_Delete(resp->body);\n        resp->body = NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (resp->error) {\n        sc_error_destroy(resp->error);\n        resp->error = NULL;\n    }\n}\n\nint sc_service_client_call_ex(sc_service_client_t     *cli,\n                              sc_service_endp_t        endpoint,\n                              const char              *method,\n                              const char              *path,\n                              const char              *request_json,\n                              sc_service_response_t   *response)\n{\n    if (!cli || !method || !path || !response) {\n        return SC_EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    memset(response, 0, sizeof(*response));\n\n    cJSON *body_json = NULL;\n    long http_status = 0;\n\n    int rc = sc_service_client_call(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                    request_json, &body_json, &http_status);\n\n    response->http_status = http_status;\n    response->body = body_json;\n\n    if (rc != SC_OK) {\n        response->status = rc;\n        response->error = sc_error_create(SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY, rc, (int)http_status,\n                                          \"Service call failed with status %ld\", http_status);\n        return rc;\n    }\n\n    /* Check if the response body contains an error structure */\n    if (body_json) {\n        cJSON *error_obj = cJSON_GetObjectItem(body_json, \"error\");\n        if (error_obj && cJSON_IsObject(error_obj)) {\n            cJSON *code = cJSON_GetObjectItem(error_obj, \"code\");\n            cJSON *domain = cJSON_GetObjectItem(error_obj, \"domain\");\n            cJSON *message = cJSON_GetObjectItem(error_obj, \"message\");\n\n            int err_code = code && cJSON_IsNumber(code) ? code->valueint : SC_ERR_UNKNOWN;\n            const char* err_domain = domain && cJSON_IsString(domain) ? domain->valuestring : \"unknown\";\n            const char* err_msg = message && cJSON_IsString(message) ? message->valuestring : \"Unknown error\";\n\n            /* Map domain string back to enum */\n            sc_service_domain_t domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN;\n            if (strcmp(err_domain, \"palette-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE;\n            else if (strcmp(err_domain, \"texture-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_TEXTURE;\n            else if (strcmp(err_domain, \"audio-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_AUDIO;\n            else if (strcmp(err_domain, \"narrative-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_NARRATIVE;\n\n            response->error = sc_error_create(domain_enum, err_code, (int)http_status,\n                                              \"%s\", err_msg);\n            response->status = err_code;\n            return err_code;\n        }\n    }\n\n    response->status = SC_OK;\n    return SC_OK;\n}\n```\n\n#### Changes to `api-gateway/src/rest/fallback_handlers.c`\n\nUpdate to use structured errors:\n\n```c\n#include \"sc_errors.h\"\n\n/* Build error response from sc_error_t */\nstatic char*\nbuild_error_from_sc_error(const sc_error_t *err)\n{\n    if (!err) {\n        return build_error_payload(500, \"INTERNAL_ERROR\", \"Unknown error occurred\");\n    }\n\n    char iso[32];\n    iso8601_now(iso);\n\n    cJSON *root  = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    cJSON *error = cJSON_AddObjectToObject(root, \"error\");\n\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(error, \"code\", err->http_status_code);\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(error, \"type\", sc_err_str(err->code));\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(error, \"domain\", sc_error_get_domain_name(err->domain));\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(error, \"message\", err->message ? err->message : \"No details\");\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(error, \"timestamp\", iso);\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(error, \"gateway\", SC_GATEWAY_VERSION);\n\n    char *json = cJSON_PrintUnformatted(root);\n    cJSON_Delete(root);\n    return json;\n}\n\n/* New handler for service errors */\nRestResponse\nrest_fallback_service_error(const RestRequest *req,\n                            const sc_error_t  *err)\n{\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"[REST] Service error handler for URI '%s': %s\",\n              req->uri, err ? err->message : \"null error\");\n\n    char *payload = build_error_from_sc_error(err);\n\n    RestResponse res = {\n        .status_code  = err ? err->http_status_code : 500,\n        .content_type = MIME_APP_JSON,\n        .body         = payload,\n        .body_len     = strlen(payload),\n        .must_free    = true\n    };\n    return res;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. **state)\n{\n    (void)state;\n    sc_error_t *err = NULL;\n\n    /* WHEN passing a NULL texture to serializer */\n    char *blob = NULL;\n    err = texture_service_serialize_texture(NULL, &blob);\n\n    /* THEN serialization must fail with a deterministic error */\n    assert_null(blob);\n    assert_non_null(err);\n    assert_int_equal(err->domain, SC_DOMAIN_TEXTURE);\n    assert_int_equal(err->code, SC_ERR_NULL_POINTER);\n    assert_int_equal(err->http_status_code, 400);\n\n    sc_error_destroy(err);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Memory Management Guidelines\n\n### Creating Errors\n\nErrors are created using `sc_error_create()` which allocates:\n1. The `sc_error_t` struct itself\n2. A copy of the formatted message string\n\n```c\nsc_error_t* err = sc_error_create(SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE, SC_ERR_DATA_NOT_FOUND, 404,\n                                  \"Palette with id %s not found\", id);\n```\n\n### Destroying Errors\n\nErrors must be destroyed when no longer needed to prevent memory leaks:\n\n```c\nif (err != NULL) {\n    /* Handle the error */\n    log_error(\"Error: %s\", err->message);\n\n    /* Destroy when done */\n    sc_error_destroy(err);\n}\n```\n\n### Error Propagation\n\nWhen propagating errors up the call stack, the caller takes ownership:\n\n```c\nsc_error_t* do_operation(void) {\n    sc_error_t* err = some_lower_level_call();\n    if (err != NULL) {\n        /* Add context and return - caller now owns the error */\n        return err;\n    }\n    return NULL;  /* Success */\n}\n```\n\n### Error Handling Pattern\n\nThe recommended pattern for consuming functions:\n\n```c\nvoid handle_request(Request* req, Response* resp) {\n    sc_error_t* err = service_operation(req->data);\n\n    if (err != NULL) {\n        /* Use error details to build response */\n        resp->status = err->http_status_code;\n        resp->body = format_error_json(err);\n\n        /* Destroy the error after use */\n        sc_error_destroy(err);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Success path */\n    resp->status = 200;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## tests\nctest --output-on-failure\n```\n\nOr using the build script:\n\n```bash\n./scripts/build.sh --all --verbose\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary of Changes by File\n\n| File | Changes The refactoring involves:\n\n1. **Enhancing the Common Error Abstraction** in `libs/sc_common/`\n2. **Refactoring the Palette Service** (`services/palette-service/`)\n3. **Refactoring the Texture Service** (`services/texture-service/`)\n4. **Updating the API Gateway** (`api-gateway/`)\n5. **Updating Unit Tests**\n\n## Analysis of Existing Error Handling\n\n### Current State\n\nAfter analyzing the codebase, I found the following error handling patterns:\n\n1. **sc_common library** (`libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h`):\n   - Already has an `sc_error_t` struct with `code`, `sys_err`, `msg`, `file`, `line`\n   - Has an `sc_err_e` enumeration with comprehensive error codes\n   - Has an `sc_err_to_http()` function that maps error codes to HTTP status codes\n   - Uses macros like `SC_RAISE()` and `SC_TRY()` for error propagation\n   - **Missing**: No service domain identification, no factory functions for dynamic error creation\n\n2. **Palette Service** (`services/palette-service/`):\n   - Uses `bool` return values for most functions\n   - Has service-specific status enum `sc_ps_status_t` in the public header\n   - Repository uses `palette_repo_status_t` enum\n   - No unified error message propagation\n\n3. **Texture Service** (`services/texture-service/`):\n   - Uses `texture_service_status_t` enum\n   - Repository uses integer codes (`REPO_OK`, `REPO_ERR_*`)\n   - No unified error message propagation\n\n4. **API Gateway** (`api-gateway/`):\n   - Service client uses simple integer return codes\n   - Fallback handlers generate JSON error responses manually\n   - No structured consumption of service errors\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### 1. Enhanced Common Error Abstraction\n\n#### Changes to `libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h`\n\nAdd the following after the existing includes and before the error enumeration:\n\n```c\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Service domain enumeration\n * Identifies the originating service for unified error handling across\n * the microservice constellation.\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef enum {\n    SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN = 0,\n    SC_DOMAIN_COMMON,         /* Common library errors */\n    SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE,        /* Palette service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_TEXTURE,        /* Texture service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_AUDIO,          /* Audio service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_NARRATIVE,      /* Narrative service */\n    SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY         /* API Gateway */\n} sc_service_domain_t;\n```\n\nModify the existing `sc_error_t` struct to include domain and HTTP status:\n\n```c\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Structured error object (enhanced)\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\ntypedef struct sc_error {\n    sc_err_e             code;            /* High level error code */\n    int                  sys_err;         /* Underlying errno / subsystem code */\n    sc_service_domain_t  domain;          /* Originating service domain */\n    int                  http_status_code;/* Recommended HTTP status code */\n    char                *message;         /* Dynamically allocated, human-readable text */\n    const char          *file;            /* Source file where error was raised */\n    int                  line;            /* Line in source file */\n} sc_error_t;\n\n#define SC_ERROR_INIT { \\\n    .code = SC_OK, \\\n    .sys_err = 0, \\\n    .domain = SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN, \\\n    .http_status_code = 200, \\\n    .message = NULL, \\\n    .file = NULL, \\\n    .line = 0 \\\n}\n```\n\nAdd factory and destructor function declarations:\n\n```c\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Error factory and destructor functions\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/**\n * sc_error_create\n *\n * Factory function to create a new sc_error_t instance with all fields populated.\n * The message is formatted using printf-style variadic arguments.\n *\n * @param domain      The originating service domain\n * @param code        The service-specific error code\n * @param http_status The recommended HTTP status code to return\n * @param format      Printf-style format string for the error message\n * @param ...         Format arguments\n *\n * @return Pointer to a heap-allocated sc_error_t, or NULL on allocation failure.\n *         Caller must free with sc_error_destroy().\n */\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                            int code,\n                            int http_status,\n                            const char* format, ...) SC_ATTR_FORMAT(printf, 4, 5);\n\n/**\n * sc_error_create_v\n *\n * Variadic list version of sc_error_create for internal use.\n */\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create_v(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                              int code,\n                              int http_status,\n                              const char* format,\n                              va_list args);\n\n/**\n * sc_error_destroy\n *\n * Frees all resources associated with an sc_error_t instance.\n * Safe to call with NULL.\n *\n * @param err The error to destroy\n */\nvoid sc_error_destroy(sc_error_t* err);\n\n/**\n * sc_error_get_domain_name\n *\n * Returns a human-readable string for the service domain.\n */\nconst char* sc_error_get_domain_name(sc_service_domain_t domain);\n```\n\n#### Implementation in `libs/sc_common/src/sc_errors.c`\n\nAdd implementations after the existing code:\n\n```c\n/* ============================================================================\n * Domain name lookup table\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nstatic const char* const g_domain_names[] = {\n    [SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN]   = \"unknown\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_COMMON]    = \"common\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE]   = \"palette-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_TEXTURE]   = \"texture-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_AUDIO]     = \"audio-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_NARRATIVE] = \"narrative-service\",\n    [SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY]   = \"api-gateway\"\n};\n\nconst char* sc_error_get_domain_name(sc_service_domain_t domain)\n{\n    if (domain < 0 || domain > SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY) {\n        return \"invalid-domain\";\n    }\n    return g_domain_names[domain];\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n * Error factory implementation\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create_v(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                              int code,\n                              int http_status,\n                              const char* format,\n                              va_list args)\n{\n    sc_error_t* err = calloc(1, sizeof(sc_error_t));\n    if (!err) {\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    err->domain = domain;\n    err->code = (sc_err_e)code;\n    err->http_status_code = http_status;\n    err->sys_err = errno;  /* Capture current errno */\n    err->file = NULL;\n    err->line = 0;\n\n    /* Format the message if format string provided */\n    if (format != NULL) {\n        va_list args_copy;\n        va_copy(args_copy, args);\n        int len = vsnprintf(NULL, 0, format, args_copy);\n        va_end(args_copy);\n\n        if (len > 0) {\n            err->message = malloc((size_t)len + 1);\n            if (err->message) {\n                vsnprintf(err->message, (size_t)len + 1, format, args);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return err;\n}\n\nsc_error_t* sc_error_create(sc_service_domain_t domain,\n                            int code,\n                            int http_status,\n                            const char* format, ...)\n{\n    va_list args;\n    va_start(args, format);\n    sc_error_t* err = sc_error_create_v(domain, code, http_status, format, args);\n    va_end(args);\n    return err;\n}\n\nvoid sc_error_destroy(sc_error_t* err)\n{\n    if (err == NULL) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Free the dynamically allocated message */\n    if (err->message != NULL) {\n        free(err->message);\n        err->message = NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Free the error struct itself */\n    free(err);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. *body;           /* Response body (JSON) */\n    sc_error_t *error;          /* Error details if status != SC_OK */\n} sc_service_response_t;\n\n/**\n * Free a service response\n */\nvoid sc_service_response_free(sc_service_response_t *resp);\n\n/**\n * Enhanced call function that returns structured errors\n */\nint sc_service_client_call_ex(sc_service_client_t     *cli,\n                              sc_service_endp_t        endpoint,\n                              const char              *method,\n                              const char              *path,\n                              const char              *request_json,\n                              sc_service_response_t   *response);\n```\n\n#### Changes to `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c`\n\n```c\n#include \"sc_errors.h\"\n\nvoid sc_service_response_free(sc_service_response_t *resp)\n{\n    if (!resp) return;\n\n    if (resp->body) {\n        cJSON_Delete(resp->body);\n        resp->body = NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (resp->error) {\n        sc_error_destroy(resp->error);\n        resp->error = NULL;\n    }\n}\n\nint sc_service_client_call_ex(sc_service_client_t     *cli,\n                              sc_service_endp_t        endpoint,\n                              const char              *method,\n                              const char              *path,\n                              const char              *request_json,\n                              sc_service_response_t   *response)\n{\n    if (!cli || !method || !path || !response) {\n        return SC_EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    memset(response, 0, sizeof(*response));\n\n    cJSON *body_json = NULL;\n    long http_status = 0;\n\n    int rc = sc_service_client_call(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                    request_json, &body_json, &http_status);\n\n    response->http_status = http_status;\n    response->body = body_json;\n\n    if (rc != SC_OK) {\n        response->status = rc;\n        response->error = sc_error_create(SC_DOMAIN_GATEWAY, rc, (int)http_status,\n                                          \"Service call failed with status %ld\", http_status);\n        return rc;\n    }\n\n    /* Check if the response body contains an error structure */\n    if (body_json) {\n        cJSON *error_obj = cJSON_GetObjectItem(body_json, \"error\");\n        if (error_obj && cJSON_IsObject(error_obj)) {\n            cJSON *code = cJSON_GetObjectItem(error_obj, \"code\");\n            cJSON *domain = cJSON_GetObjectItem(error_obj, \"domain\");\n            cJSON *message = cJSON_GetObjectItem(error_obj, \"message\");\n\n            int err_code = code && cJSON_IsNumber(code) ? code->valueint : SC_ERR_UNKNOWN;\n            const char* err_domain = domain && cJSON_IsString(domain) ? domain->valuestring : \"unknown\";\n            const char* err_msg = message && cJSON_IsString(message) ? message->valuestring : \"Unknown error\";\n\n            /* Map domain string back to enum */\n            sc_service_domain_t domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_UNKNOWN;\n            if (strcmp(err_domain, \"palette-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE;\n            else if (strcmp(err_domain, \"texture-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_TEXTURE;\n            else if (strcmp(err_domain, \"audio-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_AUDIO;\n            else if (strcmp(err_domain, \"narrative-service\") == 0) domain_enum = SC_DOMAIN_NARRATIVE;\n\n            response->error = sc_error_create(domain_enum, err_code, (int)http_status,\n                                              \"%s\", err_msg);\n            response->status = err_code;\n            return err_code;\n        }\n    }\n\n    response->status = SC_OK;\n    return SC_OK;\n}\n```\n\n#### Changes to `api-gateway/src/rest/fallback_handlers.c`\n\nUpdate to use structured errors:\n\n```c\n#include \"sc_errors.h\"\n\n/* Build error response from sc_error_t */\nstatic char*\nbuild_error_from_sc_error(const sc_error_t *err)\n{\n    if (!err) {\n        return build_error_payload(500, \"INTERNAL_ERROR\", \"Unknown error occurred\");\n    }\n\n    char iso[32];\n    iso8601_now(iso);\n\n    cJSON *root  = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    cJSON *error = cJSON_AddObjectToObject(root, \"error\");\n\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(error, \"code\", err->http_status_code);\n *json = cJSON_PrintUnformatted(root);\n    cJSON_Delete(root);\n    return json;\n}\n\n/* New handler for service errors */\nRestResponse\nrest_fallback_service_error(const RestRequest *req,\n                            const sc_error_t  *err)\n{\n    LOG_DEBUG(\"[REST] Service error handler for URI '%s': %s\",\n              req->uri, err ? err->message : \"null error\");\n\n    char *payload = build_error_from_sc_error(err);\n\n    RestResponse res = {\n        .status_code  = err ? err->http_status_code : 500,\n        .content_type = MIME_APP_JSON,\n        .body         = payload,\n        .body_len     = strlen(payload),\n        .must_free    = true\n    };\n    return res;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Errors\n\nErrors are created using `sc_error_create()` which allocates:\n1. The `sc_error_t` struct itself\n2. A copy of the formatted message string\n\n```c\nsc_error_t* err = sc_error_create(SC_DOMAIN_PALETTE, SC_ERR_DATA_NOT_FOUND, 404,\n                                  \"Palette with id %s not found\", id);\n```\n\n### Destroying Errors\n\nErrors must be destroyed when no longer needed to prevent memory leaks:\n\n```c\nif (err != NULL) {\n    /* Handle the error */\n    log_error(\"Error: %s\", err->message);\n\n    /* Destroy when done */\n    sc_error_destroy(err);\n}\n```\n\n### Error Propagation\n\nWhen propagating errors up the call stack, the caller takes ownership:\n\n```c\nsc_error_t* do_operation(void) {\n    sc_error_t* err = some_lower_level_call();\n    if (err != NULL) {\n        /* Add context and return - caller now owns the error */\n        return err;\n    }\n    return NULL;  /* Success */\n}\n```\n\n### Error Handling Pattern\n\nThe recommended pattern for consuming functions:\n\n```c\nvoid handle_request(Request* req, Response* resp) {\n    sc_error_t* err = service_operation(req->data);\n\n    if (err != NULL) {\n        /* Use error details to build response */\n        resp->status = err->http_status_code;\n        resp->body = format_error_json(err);\n\n        /* Destroy the error after use */\n        sc_error_destroy(err);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Success path */\n    resp->status = 200;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Build Instructions\n\nAfter implementing all changes:\n\n```bash\n# From project root\nmkdir -p build && cd build\ncmake .. -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Debug -DSC_ENABLE_TESTS=ON\nmake -j$(nproc)\n\n# Run tests\nctest --output-on-failure\n```\n\nOr using the build script:\n\n```bash\n./scripts/build.sh --all --verbose\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary of Changes by File\n\n| File | Changes
